He looked around at the crowd with a smile on his face and then turned in one of Black’s legs. FIGURE FUCKING FOUR AROUND THE FOUL POLE~! The fans were on fire for THAT move, cheering like wild. Granted, it wasn’t the prettiest thing to ever be seen, but it sure as hell was innovative. Sharc had the sloppy hold locked in for a few seconds before he dropped it, landing on the dirt track on the ground. Sharc got to his feet and jumped up on the wall in celebration, as the crowd cheered Sharc on. "SHARC! SHARC! SHARC! SHARC!" Black, however, seized the opportunity, and swung around the pole, sending Sharc flying back to the ground. The crowd let out another loud "Ohhhh!" as Sharc landed hard and rolled a few feet further. "BLACK! BLACK! BLACK! BLACK!" the fans countered their own previous chant. Black jumped down off the wall, favoring his left leg just a bit after the figure-four. Sharc was back up and charged Black, but Black ducked down and seamlessly grabbed hold of Sharc’s neck and dropped down, delivering a snapping neckbreaker. After getting back up again, the Black and Sharc brawled themselves toward a technical area. Black and Sharc exchanged some punches, before Black scooped up a television monitor and cracked Sharc in the midsection with it. But Black made a mistake in dropping it, because Sharc grabbed hold of Black’s hair and hit a face jam ala Syxx-Pac on the monitor. Sharc began knocking audio equipment off of a table and knocked the legs out. He picked up the table and suplexed it onto Black. Not a lot of impact, obviously, but it got a bit of a pop from the crowd. Sharc then began walking right toward the aisle. He looked up at the gigantic Blongitron and set up the table beneath it. He turned around, intending to go find Black, but discovered Black had already made his way toward Sharc, who got locked into a Blackplex — the belly to belly double underhook suplex. Both men laid on the ground for several seconds after that move, getting their wind and trying to get some much needed energy to keep the pace of this one up. Black was the first to get up. He pulled Sharc slowly to his feet and scooped him up. Sharc kicked his way free, feeling he was about to get too well acquainted with a table, and slipped behind Black. Sharc grabbed Black’s right arm, spun him around, kicked him in the nuts and blasted him with an implant DDT on the ground. Again, several seconds passed after that move. This time, Sharc was up first, and he dragged Black until he was set up on the table below the big ‘tron. Sharc landed a few more punches to Black’s face, just to make sure he wouldn’t be going anywhere, and then made the long climb up to the base of the Blonger. Once there, Sharc walked out along the thin plank in front of the big flashing monitor until he was directly above the table, about 10 to 15 feet above Black. The fans roared and cheered Sharc on, knowing they were about to see something highlight-worthy. SHARC DOVE! *CRUNCH!* "OHHHHH!" Black was crushed and the table obliterated upon the impact of the suicide splash! "HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT!" A pair of officials from the area rushed over to check on both men, to see if both had survived in one piece. Sharc rolled away of his own power, and Black began kicking his feet and clutching at his back. Both men were easily down for more than half a minute before either even started to get up. The fans, meanwhile, were treated to various replays of the suicidal dive by Sharc. Eventually, Sharc was up. The brawl, you’d think, would head back toward the ring. But that is not quite what happened. Instead, Sharc tossed Black into the crowd for a second time. Black began to show signs of life as he was dragged past the rabid Action fans, throwing left-handed punches, but Sharc responded with rights of his own for each blow. They were heading towards the dugout. Once there, Black surprised Sharc, pushing him chest first into the wall. Unfazed, Sharc climbed up on top of the dugout roof, with Black following close behind. Black blocked a punch by Sharc and responded with a left-handed punch. Then a headbutt. Then another headbutt. And a third. Sharc fell straight backward with a loud thud. The crowd urged Black on now. They were taking this fight to every corner of the park, giving every fan their money’s worth tonight. Black scooped Sharc up and hooked him. HIPTOSS ON THE DUGOUT ROOF! Sharc roared out in pain upon sudden and violent impact on that one. But Sharc quickly pushed himself up, wanting to get immediate revenge. Bad move. Sharc was caught and the toll on his back became even greater with an Arn Anderson-style spinebuster slam! The fans were so evenly split on the two men it was hard to tell who they wanted to win. Black moved in to further assault Sharc’s back, this time deciding to lock in a submission move, even though they weren’t in the ring and it wouldn’t matter if Sharc tapped or submitted up here. That move? The Crossbow. Black locked up Sharc’s legs and combined that with a double backhanded crossface, and say hello to pain, Mr. Hertz. Sharc banged on the dugout roof with his fists, obviously in a LOT of pain here. His often-injured back was taking a severe pounding every second he was locked in this move. And it seemed like an eternity to Sharc already, even though it had only been a matter of seconds. He knew he could bang the roof without fear of submission. It distracted him a bit from the pain, but not enough. Essentially, Black had just gotten Sharc to tap out, even though it wouldn’t count for anything. Sharc had never tapped out before — although in fairness, this wasn’t a ring, so if they were in the ring, he might not have been tapping like crazy. A severe twinge in Black’s back, however, stopped him from continuing to torment Sharc. He stood up and bent his body, trying to work out the kink. Sharc rolled onto his back, still moaning in pain from the damage The Crossbow had done to him. But Sharc, even though he hurt, had always fed off pain to an extent. Now was no different. Black came back toward Sharc, who pushed himself up just enough to hit an effective dropkick to Black’s shins and take him down. Acting as quickly as he could despite his pain, now Sharc locked up Black’s legs in a deathlock and then leaned all the way back and began viciously pulling up on Black’s chin. The Spinebreaker. Payback’s a bitch, motherfucker. This time, Black was banging crazily on the dugout roof. Same situation, if this had been in the ring, it’d be a tap out, but up here, it was releasing loads and loads and pain and frustration. After several seconds in the hold, Sharc was forced, due to his own pain, to drop the hold. Now he had to stretch, trying to get his back feeling proper. Not that it ever would for the rest of his life, but at least good enough to continue on in this fight. Both men laid on the roof of the dugout for several more seconds before they began crawling toward the edge and hopping down into the steps below. There, the brawl resumed, and the fight seemed ready to head back to the ring at last. They brawled through the crowd, tossing punches at each other along the way, but more or less just trying to get back to ringside now. Both men, looking exhausted, collapsed over the railing back at ringside. Sharc dragged himself toward the ring and went beneath it. He pulled out... Yes, a LADDER. After all, why do anything stupid now? Sharc headed over toward the announce tables. You better believe it. He leaned the ladder against the steel post and went back for the Rook. The two traded more weak punches and Sharc dragged Black along, Destination: France. Well, the French announce table anyway. Sharc punched and punched and punched Black some more until he fell backward onto the table. Did Sharc take anything off that table? Monitors? Hell no. Sharc did NOT move a damn thing off that table. If he could, he would have put MORE shit on that table just so it would hurt Black more when he dove off the top of the ladder and crushed the fuck out of him. And he would have done just that, except, he really wasn’t thinking clearly. Sharc’s brain was working at a very simple level right now: Ladder goes up. Sharc jumps. Black gets smooshy. So, with that elaborate plan laid out in his mind, Sharc set up the ladder and began climbing. His legs wouldn’t move him as fast as he wanted to and it was pissing him off. It seemed like it took him five minutes per rung, even though it was only a few seconds on each. Black got off the table. Sharc was about three rungs away from the top when he noticed the ladder began rocking. He looked down and saw Black’s face looking up at him. Sharc did the only thing he could do...wait. Black ascended quickly, and before Sharc even could register it, he was the recipient of a Rook Black one of a kind punch to the nose. And even more quickly than that, Black reached over the top of the ladder and hooked up Sharc’s arm. And then his leg. OH. MY. FUCKING! GOD! SUPA! FISHAMAN! BUSTA! TO HELL! The French announce table exploded in about 20 different directions and the crowd popped HUGE as both men’s bodies did free falls into crash landings. "HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT!" How do you learn to fall? On the job fucking training, buddy. That’s the answer. But that’s not even the real question. It’s how the hell do you get up AFTER the fall. And right now, neither man was. Instead, Action viewers got to view the move over and over again, seeing Sharc being driven from the top of the ladder all the way down on his upper shoulders. Black didn’t get off much better as he crashed through the table as well. Amazingly, both men avoided the major problem of landing on monitors, which could have seriously fucked them up good. A good minute went by. As did another before both men slowly began to move out from the disaster area. Black was crawling on his way to the ring first. Sharc was still struggling a bit to get his senses back. But in a few more seconds, he sat up and began crawling on hands and knees toward the ring, trailing Black by several feet. Black pulled himself inside and forced himself onto his feet with aid from the ropes. He got a mock cheer from the crowd, glad to see the match actually ready to resume IN THE RING for a change. Sharc pulled himself onto the apron and slowly rolled the rest of the way into the ring. Black got into "come on, get up" position, waiting on Sharc to do just that so he could deliver what may be the final move in this brutal match. Sharc grabbed hold of the ropes and pushed himself up. He staggered toward Black, who locked in Sharc. GAUNTLET THROWDOWN! Black hit EVERY BIT OF IT! The crowd exploded in cheers and jumped to their feet, convinced that was the death blow to Sharc. They cheered on Black as he hooked Sharc’s leg for the cover! ONE!! TWO!! THREE!! NO!! SHARC GOT HIS SHOULDER UP JUST IN TIME! Rook continued to lay on top of Sharc, in complete disbelief. From somewhere very far away, Black could swear he heard somebody explaining that Sharc had gotten his shoulder up and the match was continuing. Black was just surprised that Sharc was still alive after the fisherman buster through the announce table more than anything else. He was sure THAT would have done it. Black got up. Too much thinking would not help Black here. He had to ‘do.’ And that do involved heading up to the top rope right now and connecting with a moonsault. Unfortunately for Black, Sharc rolled out of the way of the moonsault, and Black hit perfectly on the mat, jarring him from head to toe. Sharc continued rolling, all the way to the apron, and then collapsed to his knees on the floor and began digging around under the ring again. Black followed Sharc out to the floor once he had recovered from his miss in the ring. All he saw when he landed on the ground were Sharc’s ankles. So Black began pulling Sharc out. He suddenly roared out in pain as Sharc rammed a fire extinguisher into the lower half of his left leg, the same leg victimized by the figure-four earlier in the match. Sharc came out from under the ring and rammed the end of the fire extinguisher into Black’s face! Black fell backward, unmoving. Sharc tossed the weapon onto the apron of the ring and then quickly tried to pull Black up and get him into the ring. It proved very tough, with Black being little more than dead weight after the cheap tactic. The fans had gasped, but the move didn’t stop them from cheering Sharc on, amazingly. They pretty much expecting some dirty tactics from Sharc, no matter which "side" he was on at the time. Sharc sighed in frustration and left Black on the floor. He went back under the ring and retrieved a table. That proved easy enough to get into the ring. Sharc headed inside and set the table up, then headed back to the floor. Black had a bit more sense about him now and proved easier to get into the ring. Back inside, Sharc began digging into his right boot. The fans looked on, wondering what the hell he could have snuck into the match. They didn’t have to wait long to see: A bottle of lighter fluid! Sharc went to the table and began dousing the top with the fluid. He emptied the entire container on the wood and then tossed the container out of the ring. Then he produced a lighter from his other boot. The referee, knowing he had little hope of stopping Sharc, dove for the fire extinguisher. The crowd was buzzing, knowing they were about to see somebody take a highly-dangerous spot, as both men were putting their bodies and careers on the line like never before. Sharc pulled Black over to him and stuck him between his legs. Black was on his knees, too exhausted and hurt to be able to stand there. Sharc flicked the lighter and tossed it on the table! *WHOOSH* The table was up in flames. LOW BLOW BY BLACK! Black put Sharc between his legs and jacked him up! POWERBOMB THROUGH THE FLAMING TABLE! "HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT!" The referee began shooting both men with the fire extinguisher the second Sharc went through the table and began spasming on the mat and roaring out in pain. The move minimized the damage to Sharc, but it also ended up hurting Black, as he was temporarily blinded and couldn’t move in for the kill. After several seconds, however, Black was able to regain his bearings and realize he had the biggest chance to win right here. He hopped on top of the singed Sharc and made the cover! ONE! TWO! THREE! The crowd EXPLODED in cheers for Black, only to see the referee pointing repeatedly to Sharc’s right leg, which he somehow had the presence of mind to draped over the bottom rope! Black collapsed flat on the mat. He had been sure that was it. But AGAIN, Sharc saved himself as the jaws of defeat. Black put his hands to his face, entirely frustrated by his inability to put Sharc away and become Action’s impact Player for the coming year. Victory was so close at hand, it may only take one more big move to get Sharc dead and buried. And Black knew it. He dragged Sharc slowly up to his shaky feet and headed behind Sharc, ready to hit Checkmate ’99, but Sharc sensed the immediate danger closing in and starting driving his elbow viciously into Black’s midsection, then some hard punches. Sharc quickly scooted behind Black now and grabbed him around the throat! The fans all stood up in unison as Sharc, with one last mighty burst of energy, grabbed hold of Black’s tights and jacked Black straight up in the air and dropped him straight down on top of his head! BLOOD IN THE WATER! The crowd exploded in cheers again as Sharc’s ultimate finishing move — the inverted brainbuster — connected in all its brutal glory. Black crumpled to the mat, appearing now that HE was all but done in this contest, even after taking big time control in the later stages. Sharc didn’t move in for the cover right away however, remaining seated mid-ring, in a complete fog, staring down at the patter on the canvas, as if hypnotized. The crowd, eventually, popped something in his head, and he looked around, realizing the match was NOT over. He grabbed hold of Black’s pants and rolled him onto his back. Sharc laid back on Black! ONE! TWO! THREE! NO!! Black somehow got HIS shoulder up! The bad cover by Sharc no doubt helped keep Black alive here. The fans clapped and cheered, knowing that both men needed some inspiration to bring this epic battle to a conclusion. Sharc responded first, pushing himself to his feet. He dragged Black up to his feet, ready to drive him into the mat with a DDT, but Black reversed it into a Northern lights suplex and held on with a bridge! ONE! TWO! THRE-KICKOUT BY SHARC! The fans again sighed in a mixture of both relief and disappointment, as the split crowd really didn’t know what to cheer, other than the action at this point. After a few seconds of rest, Black pushed himself up and ran towards Sharc, connecting with a weak clothesline that sent both men into the corner. Black ducked down and drove his shoulder hard into Sharc’s midsection. And again. And again. The next time, he lifted Sharc onto the top turnbuckle and hit a stiff punch to Sharc’s jaw. Black was going for the final blow. Checkmate '03 from the top rope. He turned around and backed himself onto the second rope and grabbed hold of Sharc’s arms, ready to take Sharc on his last ride for the evening. The fans all stood up, reserving their cheers for the final impact, but buzzing nonetheless. But Sharc suddenly ripped his right arm free of Black’s grasp. Using Black to support himself, Sharc did a quick rollaround on the top turnbuckle support beam and wrapped his left arm under Black’s jaw... AND DOVE STRAIGHT DOWN TO THE MAT! SHARC ATTACK FROM THE TOP ROPE~! Without any delay this time, Sharc dragged Black onto his back, made the proper cover and hooked Black’s left leg! ONE! ... TWO! ... THREE!! No feet on the ropes. No shoulders up. The bell sounded, and Sharc had won this brutal and amazing brawl. Outcome: Sharc via Pinfall; Action’s First Player. ---- Burning Sensation. Burning Sensation. Starring: Sharc & Levine. Writer: Paul L & John L. The five senses kicked in. Hearing the roar of the crowd sent a chill up his spine. Feeling the aches and pains suddenly vanish into thin air, for adrenaline had begun to pump through his veins once more. Seeing the bright lights flashing in his eyes surrounding his body in glimmers of radiance. Smelling the slow hot air existing only because of ultimate physical fatigue and extreme sweat. And lastly, tasting the sweet taste of victory. Richard Hertz, known to the wrestling world as Sharc, lifted his head slightly and peered over at his fallen opponent. He let out a faint, yet satisfied, smile as he arched his torso to an upright position and scanned the crowd vaguely. He began to view the entire crowd, turning in place and walking in a small circle as he scanned the audience that applauded him so joyously. Sharc felt the grasp of the official wrap around his wrist and lift his arm up high. And at that moment, for the first time in Sharc’s career in Action Wrestling…he felt a sense of pride. Pride in knowing he had ventured off into becoming the best wrestler in Action Wrestling. He was once a man that was kicked down for his hardcore abilities, but tonight they would now have to recognize him as a wrestler… And not just any wrestler…but the wrestler who won the Players and Pawns tournament. Victory finally set in for Sharc, allowing him to lift his free hand up above his head and celebrate his victory. In Action Wrestling, no one would’ve suspected Sharc to capture the victory…but he did. A man with a heart so black that it could be compared to the dead of night; a man with no remorse nor emotion for anyone other than Amy King; a man who burned his recent victim alive and killed everything known about him…was the victor. A horrid man once said that Sharc was a false representation of “Mr. Action Wrestling”. But tonight, even if it was for just this moment, he truly represented the federation that he wrestled in. Sharc soon fell to his knees and raised both clenched fists into the air one last time, allowing the fame of victory to pour around him. The group of officials who stood watch over the new Players & Pawns trophy, on which Sharc’s name would be inscribed, now removed the prize from it’s case as "The Riches" by Jane’s Addiction hit the speakers. The entrance music of Action’s owner, Reed Young. The idiot was on his way down the aisle. Sharc leaned back on the ropes as a table was set-up mid-ring and the suited fellows placed Sharc’s prize upon it. They were all wearing gloves, so not to get any finger prints on it. Then they stood behind the table and waited on Young to walk the long, long aisle. The trophy was silver and shaped, fittingly enough, like the king from chess. It wasn’t as big as the National Hockey League’s Stanley Cup prize, awarded to the best team in hockey every summer, but it was of good size. The trophy definitely had some weight to it. The ring was filled with other officials and photographers, who snapped photos of the victorious Sharc, pictures that would be posted in magazines and on the Internet, to further him along and give his name more recognition than any other event had in his life. More than anything he’d accomplished in any other federation. Sharc had arrived. He was a player. The only thing from keeping it official was having Reed Young present the trophy. As Reed Young slowly lifted the trophy up in his hand, grabbing a microphone securely in his free hand, he smiled widely and nodded at the crowd in attendance whom began to stand and clap for the man who had won the tournament. Young lowered his microphone, and pressing his idiotic tendencies aside, allowed Sharc to bask in the glory of victory. Sharc, leaning against the ropes, scanned the crowd that roared in approval…and in reaction, the new Action representative raised a closed fist in the air and pushed himself off the ropes and balanced himself on his feet. With a pleased smile over his face, and a closed fist of victory raised above his head, he flipped his red hair behind his head and looked to walk over to his trophy prize. But something stopped him… He suddenly heard the crowd yelling out warnings…he saw Reed Young’s eyes widen in petrified fear…and he heard a loud grunting sound behind him. Before Sharc’s face could turn to face the opposition, he felt cold hard brass knock into the back of his skull. His eyes slowly rolled to the back of his head, as he felt his body crash onto the mat floor. Reed Young screamed like a little school girl and quickly dropped the trophy in hand and began to scurry up the ramp. The fans jeered loudly for Sharc’s assailant…a man not even signed by Action Wrestling…Levine. Levine, who would usually bask in the glory of hatred, didn’t pay any attention the crowd that jeered him and just began to stomp wildly into Sharc’s body. Sharc attempted to power his way up, sweat and blood drizzling down his face, but it was all in vain after a few stomps later when Levine would pound Sharc back onto the mat. Sharc lifted his head for a moment, but only received a STIFF boot to the back of his head, sending his face crashing into the mat. Blood flowed freely from Rich’s nose, as his nose crushed in between his face and the mat. Sharc now had a crimson moustache to match his crimson skull. He soon rolled to his stomach, opening his eyes a small bit to get the figure of his assailant correctly. But when he found out, there was nothing he could do. His muscles weren’t moving, his mind wasn’t together, and his energy was depleted. Levine had attacked Sharc at his most vulnerable state…after a hard fought match. Levine didn’t stop the assault and just began to pummel into Sharc as hard as he could, every stomp causing bruises to form on Sharc’s pale body. A flurry of stomps to the chest caused Sharc’s chest to redden. A swift kick to the ribs caused a large black and blue mark to stain his pale white skin. But the moment where Sharc knew he was in trouble was the moment his jaw had almost snapped clear off his face. Sharc had lifted his head, only for a few moments. Levine grunted loudly, behind the bandages that covered nearly his entire face. Sharc couldn’t help but compare Levine to a mummy…but one that was quicker and much more deadly. A man he thought was dead for life, a man he thought he had relinquished from the wrestling world…was right in front of his face. Blood began to stain Sharc’s vision. As Sharc’s vision turned to one with a red tint, he watched as Levine charged forward, reared his foot forward and field goal kicked Sharc’s jaw as hard as he could. The fans let out an “OH~!” as Sharc’s head snapped back and fell to the mat, knocking him into an unconscious state. Levine stood over Sharc, watching as the blood caressed his entire face…making his face nothing more than a bloody pulp. Levine’s long leather coat danced behind him, as he stood still for a moment. His face was about half covered in white sweat filled bandages, hiding the burn scars of Levine’s face. Some parts of his body (his right shoulder, left forearm and left hand) were still bandaged up, and no doubt sending pulses of pain through his body at the mere move of them. He could feel the pain, but his mind didn’t allow him to dwell upon it. Mind over a matter; the mind only feels what it wants to feel. And at this moment, he was feeling pleasure. Pleasure in watching Sharc suffer, pleasure in watching him blood and combulse unstoppably; because it was exactly what Sharc did. Sharc believed Levine was dead, buried, and never to be seen again…but vengeance can do many things; like bring a dead man back from the grave. Levine looked over at the bloody and bruised Sharc, and sent a wad of spit to his fallen body. Sharc, using the only bit of power he had left, slowly began to crawl his way over to Levine, sending punches to his stomach. Unfortunately for Sharc, he was far too weak to do any damage. Levine just looked down at Sharc, as he just flailed fists into Levine’s unharmed abdomen, with an emotionless (yet dark) stare. The Mind Manipulator soon grabbed Sharc by his long crimson locks and looked deep into Sharc’s blood gladden face once more before sending a stiff right fist into the corner of Sharc’s jaw, knocking him to the mat in a heavy heap. As Levine slowly rolled out of the ring and began to pull Sharc along with him, security team members began to storm past audience members and towards Levine. And how did Levine respond? … BANG~! … It echoed across the arena, as fans screamed in bloody fear. A gun shot was heard. It echoed across the entire arena, silencing the crowd in attendance and stopping the security team dead in their tracks. Levine had fired the pistol into the air, as a warning. He gave the entire arena an emotionless, and frightful, stare as he pointed the gun to Sharc’s skull and began to drag the newly crowned Players and Pawn tournament champion up the ramp. Sharc looked like a rag doll, being pulled by his head as his body dragged across the steel plated rampway. Levine had the pistol pointed directly at Sharc’s skull, as he wrapped Sharc in a headlock and continued up the ramp and through the curtains. The moment they reached the backstage area, backstage crew members scurried away from the scene in fear of being harmed; Sharc finally regained consciousness and reacted with a rake to the forehead (where the scars from the burns seemed to be the most). Levine grunted loudly and fell to his knee, as Sharc began to stumble away. But Levine wasn’t hurt for long, as he quickly reached over to Sharc and pistol whipped him across the face. Levine soon holstered his gun and stared menacingly at Sharc, as a red glow began to shine in his already reddened eyes. He yanked Sharc’s limp body off the ground and quickly hurled him into the wall. Sharc stumbled forward and right into the grasp of Levine. Levine quickly grabbed Sharc by his head and hurled his body into a nearby cement pole. Sharc’s head snapped back, as his body soon followed. More security looked to rush in. A group of about five security men charged in, but Levine quickly disposed of them. One guard was sent through one of the backstage doors. Another guard was pistol whipped across the face in a flash second. The third and fourth guards were both sent to the ground with simultaneous punches, while the fifth guard was hit with a devastating uppercut with the but end of the pistol. While Levine remained busy with the security guards, Sharc had found enough time to stumble away from the scene. With the warm sensation of blood oozing from his open wounds and the stinging feeling of the bruises on and in his body worsening with every move he made, he finally got to his feet and leaned up against the nearest wall, attempting to pull himself from the harm. He knew, in his mind, that his physical self was nowhere near enough power to help fend off Levine…so in reaction, he had to escape the pain. Sharc stumbled slowly down the corridor, using the wall as a railing for support. But as Sharc continued to blindly stumble away, a locker room door opened a few feet away…and Amy King charged out and towards her lover. “Oh my god,” she whispered as she quickly ripped off an article of her clothing and began to wipe the crimson liquid from his face, “You’re hurt bad, Rich. We need to get you to the hospital, right now” She suddenly snapped her head up, hearing the guards screams of terror. Sounds of shattering glass, broken wooden elements, and bodies hitting the floor with extreme velocity echoed throughout the locker room area. Sharc looked into his lover’s eyes, and noticed the sense of fear that overcame her. Levine wasn’t going to be held off long, and she had to rush him out of the building. “We have to go, Rich. We have to go, NOW,” she harshly whispered as she began to place her head and shoulder beneath Sharc’s right arm and assist him to the exit door. She tried to go as fast as she could, but it was hard to do so without making a lot of noise. She didn’t want to catch Levine’s attention. As she helped a bloody Richard Hertz towards the exit door, she had to stop. Sharc was coughing heavily, and it forced him to his knees. She knelt down beside him, looking to help him up. Her eyes remained glued on Hertz, but when Hertz lifted his head, a pool of blood stained the floor below. He was bleeding internally, this had gotten very serious. She gasped in fright, but knew she had to be strong and hurried herself in lifting him back up. Unfortunately, something had stopped her. She felt a tight grip wrapped around the back of her neck, that left her stunned and paralyzed. Sharc quickly fell back to the floor and looked up helplessly at what had happened. Levine had arrived and quickly has his one hand wrapped around the back of Amy’s neck. Amy reached over and tried to released Levine’s hard grip, but it was all in vain. Levine pulled Amy closer, so that she could literally feel his hot breath against her face… “You know, I always envied you, Hertz. It amazes me how a piece of shit like you got a whore like this to suck you off,” Levine said as he slowly brought up his right hand, to reveal the pistol secured in his grasp. He slowly brought the pistol towards Amy’s ribs and caressed her lower abdomen, “did the stinging feeling of my mark settle yet, Amy?” Amy only responded with small shrieks of terror, as Levine smiled in her general direction. As he watched the scene, Sharc was attempting to power himself up and save Amy…but everytime he pushed himself up, he would fall back down. His muscles weren’t doing what his mind was telling him. Levine peered over at Sharc, as he inched closer to Amy’s ear and ran his tongue across the outside of it. This got Sharc very angry, and forced himself to get to his knees and grab onto the wall and attempt to pull himself up. Sharc was grunted angrily, like a wild beast, behind his crimson mask…staring a hole directly at Levine. “Oh, are you jealous, Sharc? Are you afraid I’ll take something you love?...JUST LIKE YOU DID TO ME?!?!” he suddenly went from a soft, yet sadistic voice, to one that sounded possessed, “When you burned me alive, Hertz, you never thought I would come back did you? When you burned me alive, you made sure that every thing that made me who I was was gone, didn’t you? You burned my identity away, you burned my only accolade away, and you tried to burn ME away!” The voice of the possessed Levine filled the backstage area with fear and terror. “But I’ve faced the fires of hell before and I’ve always returned to live with the living; just as I have today. And do you know what brought me out of the hospital?” he asked rhetorically, “Do you know what pushed me to get up from that bed, march over to this arena, and face you? The entire time I was in that hospital bed, all I could think about was you...destroying you, to be exact. You see Sharc, I’ve always went under a rule…” “Never dish out, what you can’t take…” Amy’s eyes widened, as Levine hurled her to the wall and aimed the pistol directly to her heart. “You killed all that I lived for…” No, muttered Sharc beneath his breathe. “And I’ll kill everything you lived for…” A silenced bullet ran free from Levine’s pistol. It was as if time slowed down, as Sharc watched the pistol jerk back and the bullet travel through the air. He wanted to leap up, as if he were some sort of supernatural human being traveling through the space and time of the world. He wanted to jump into Amy and knock her out of the way, and than get up and beat the living piss out of Levine. But he couldn’t do it. The bullet ran its course into Amy’s heart. Amy looked down at her chest, and couldn’t see blood. Maybe her vision was distorted, or maybe Levine had mercy on her. She immediately erased the second choice from her mind the moment her vision began to get blurry. Her eyes began to flicker on and off, as she looked over at a shocked Richard Hertz. She opened her mouth and wanted to say something, but she couldn’t, the air had rushed out of her body as she hit the cold floor below… Levine smiled wide as he watched her body slowly fall into a paralyzed state. You killed her, Sharc mumbled as his widened eyes remained fixed on his fallen lover. You killer her, he said once again, his eyes now beginning to tear in a mixture of anger and remorse… “YOU FUCKING KILLED HER!!!!!!!!” it was the adrenaline rush he needed. It was as if all the pain in his body was forgotten, and all of his aches and pains were healed. Sharc suddenly shot up from the ground and immediately sent his fist forward into Levine’s bandaged face. Levine grunted loudly as he was sent into the wall. Sharc didn’t stop and began to throw wild fists into Levine’s face, before grabbing the Satanic God by the back of his head and sending his face into the wall. Blood escaped Levine’s nose, and began to stain his white bandages. “I’LL FUCKING KILL YOU!” Sharc shouted out maniacally. The same possessed voice Levine had used earlier was now adopted by Sharc, who had seemingly gone into a zone of pure hatred, with nothing but revenge on his mind. He now had nothing to lose, and everything to gain. Levine had killed the one thing that meant something to his life…and he had to be punished for it. He quickly wrapped his hands around Levine’s throat and hurled him into the exit door, throwing Levine right through it. Levine crashed to the cement floor of the parking lot and was quickly getting to his feet. Sharc didn’t allow it and quickly soccer kicked Levine in the chest as hard as he could. Levine hit hard, making sure to keep his grip on his pistol. Levine shot a bullet towards Sharc, but it completely missed and allowed Sharc to kick the pistol clear from Levine’s hand. Sharc was now in control, but his adrenaline was running thin and the aches began to return. But in his mind he refused to let it stop him. He soon lifted Levine to his feet and looked to punch him once again, but Levine had caught the fist in mid flight and cracked a grin at Sharc, “I hope you know…I just let you get those hits on me,” within a split second of hearing Levine’s voice…Sharc felt a quick shot to his face that sent him stumbling into a nearby car. Levine smiled sadistically, as he wrapped his hands around Sharc’s neck and proceeded to smash the back of Sharc’s skull through the rear view mirror of the car. Sharc’s head dangled like a rag doll afterwards, but Levine wasn’t done as he grabbed Sharc by the back of his bloody head and slammed Sharc’s face into the side window of the vehicle…than the other side window of the vehicle…followed by a quick uppercut to Sharc’s jaw…which caused the crimson haired victor of P&P to fall helplessly to the ground. Levine found this moment to slowly run his hand over his nose and wipe the excess blood that oozed from his nostrils. His nose may have been broken, but at this point that was not the concern of Levine. His concern was eliminating Sharc and making him feel the pain that Levine once felt while his skin boiled and scarred in a firey glow. He eyed over at his fallen pistol and walked over to it, lifting it up from his handle and emptying the bullet cartridge he had used on Amy King. Within a few seconds, he had pulled out a new cartridge from his pocket and loaded the raging bull pistol up. He slowly walked over to Sharc, like death walking over to it’s victim, like a tiger looming over it’s prey. Death was breathing down Sharc’s neck and getting closer with every step Levine took towards the fallen Sharc. Sirens could be heard in the distance, and Sharc took some sort of comfort in that. Maybe they would come in time to save him…or maybe they would be too late… Fear rushed through Sharc’s body, as Levine neared the fallen Sharc and placed his boot hard against Sharc’s throat. Hertz didn’t even have enough power to lift his hands and attempt to pull Levine’s foot off, he only had enough power to watch as his foe pulled the silver pistol forward and aimed it directly at Sharc’s head. Sharc could see the red laserlight flashing in his eyes, and he could see the menacing smile that plagued Levine’s scarred face. “Do you know the feeling of being burned, Sharc? Do you even realize the amount of pain you put me through? I was in a hospital for two whole weeks, not being able to move as they applied skin grafts to my scarred skin. And thanks to you, I can never forget you! Everytime I look in the mirror and see those FUCKING scars, I will be forced to remember who you are,” he grunted out behind clenched teeth, “Because of your fucking actions, I am forced to live my life with half of a burnt face. It’s not like these pricks in Action who I can forget about the moment I leave…you scared me for life you mother fucker…and the only way to erase you from a horrid past…is to relinquish you from this world…” Sharc’s eyes widened, as Levine clicked back on the safety of the pistol. His finger caressed the trigger as he looked down at his foe. “But when I kill you Hertz…I want the world to see it. I want everyone of those cum sucking leeches to watch their hero fail, just like he has twice before. I want them all to realize that you cannot win, and that you will die trying. If you have any balls, you’ll face me…in front of THEM…and you’ll fail like a good little bitch,” he shouted insanely, “But until you decide to fight me…I’ll give you a sample of the pain you will endure during your slow death…” He suddenly moved the pistol away from Sharc’s skull. Sharc took a deep sigh of relief for a few seconds…until he heard the pistol go off. A sudden shock of immense pain coursed his leg, as he felt a warm sensation ooze out of his left upper thigh. Sharc bellowed out in pain, his bloody cries sent a chill through viewers’ spines. He had been shot in the leg, and the burning sensation surrounding his leg made Sharc bellow out in agony. Levine soon placed the pistol away and smiled at Sharc as he cried out. Levine soon knelt beside Sharc and looked deep into his eyes. “You know, Hertz, you took my PIW Title and burned it along with me…and now I have no accolade to showcase,” Levine said with a smile, “I guess I’ll need another accolade to show to the world…” And with that…the most gruesome act was taken place to Richard Hertz. Levine had inserted two of his fingers into the open wound of Sharc. Sharc screamed out in agony, painful tears strolling down his face as he tried to grab Levine by the wrist and pull his fingers out. But Levine continued to dig in the open wound, opening the wound even further. And as Levine pulled his fingers out, he pulled out the silver bullet (now drenched in red blood). Levine looked at the bloody bullet in the light, and showed it to the fallen Sharc, who was now breathing deeply and harshly from the pain. “I think this will be a good replacement, don’t you think? Because whenever I look at it…it will always bring back…good memories” And with that, Levine took off. The sirens had grew louder, and commotion began to fill the backstage area. Levine disappeared into the night, as Sharc remained in a pool of his own blood. Sharc was breathing heavily and tried to get up and chase Levine, but the pain was far too immense and it prevented him from even moving… Hell, it even prevented him from staying awake… Everything went dark, his hearing went deaf, and his feeling went numb… ---- Sympathy for the… Guardian Devil. Wrestling “What's my name Tell me, baby, what's my name Tell me, sweetie, what's my name” Pressure Point 85 - 12/07/03 ---- ’The Original’ Jeff Garvin (A) vs. Coral Avalon ’The Original’ Jeff Garvin (A) vs. Coral Avalon Referee: Patricia Grayson. Writers: Mike R. & Michael K. D. What couldn't be said about the storied history between Action! Wrestling's Heavyweight Champion, Jeff Garvin, and the impressionable young Coral Avalon? The two men have had what was the longest-running current feud in Action! today, starting way back in September of 2002 when both men first set foot in Action! Wrestling. The two men have had three pay-per-view encounters and six matches together. Their feud started out with just these two men, and yet, they would add valets to their team, with Coral bringing in his girlfriend(at the time), Allison Lindum, and Jeff Garvin marrying and bringing in the younger sister of Joey Malone, Julie. Yet, there was a consistent pattern in the six matches that these two men have had. Coral Avalon never lost to "the Original" after dropping their first PPV match to him. Holding a 4-1-1 record over the Action! Champion, Coral Avalon has essentially had Garvin's number. He defeated Garvin at Players & Pawns in their epic iron man match that garnered Avalon and Garvin the "Match of the Year" prize. He defeated Garvin at Juggernaut II, captured his Bantam Championship, and became the longest reigning champion as a result. And, possibly most importantly of all, Coral Avalon pinned Jeff Garvin in a six man tag match back in October, proving that he still had Garvin's number. Garvin was a desperate champion, and any outside observer would have given this match to the Kleptomaniac outright. So, Garvin sought a different way to stop the onslaught of his greatest rival. He started attacking those dear to him, his best friends. Having to bear witness to this still didn't deter Coral Avalon from his mission of taking the Action Championship from Jeff Garvin, it only seemed to strengthen it. But soon, the effects took in. Coral felt the pain of guilt that his bid for Garvin's title was hurting his friends. And last week, Garvin took out Allison Lindum, the woman that Coral still loved, with the Memphis Death Certificate and the Hammer Jammer. So no, tonight wasn't about the title any more. No, tonight was about vanquishing the man that has haunted his every step since he first entered Action! Wrestling. And in an enviroment similar to what his two trainers, Joey Malone and Kent Anthason, went through at this time last year over the same championship, nobody knew what the Kleptomaniac was really capable of. Pat Hoskins stood in the ring, prepared for announcements, "Ladies and gentlemen, the following contest is the Last Man Standing contest for the Action! Wrestling Championship!" Fans erupted. "Won't Back Down" by Fuel. The fans in the Safeco Field reacted instantly by cheering loudly for the Kleptomaniac. Behind a curtain of blue, the silhouette of Coral Avalon could be seen walking towards the curtain, before a loud explosion rocked the entryway between the pawn and the rook, and the curtain dropped, revealing Avalon himself. It was the first time Coral Avalon ever had pyro involved with his entrance, so it's a pretty big moment for the Kleptomaniac. Dressed in his alternate "negative image" version of his tights, which meant that you took Coral's regular tights, changed the black to white, changed the blue to black, and changed the white to gray, you'd have a pretty good idea of what Coral's tights looked like right now. "First, the challenger," Hoskins said, as he raised his arms to indicate Avalon's arrival. Coral was all alone on this night, with his manager, Allison Lindum, still injured with an ankle sprain after the horrific Memphis Death Certificate and Hammer Jammer perpetrated by "the Original" Jeff Garvin, and he wasn't specifically too thrilled about that. Indeed, the broody, content look of Coral Avalon was not present for this night, only a look on his face which suggested that he was going to eat Jeff Garvin's soul and drink his blood. Or something like that, anyway. How any mortal man can eat another mortal's soul was a topic covered in Ruben Ross's latest book, "42 Different Ways To Prepare And Serve Human Souls". "He hails from New Orleans, Louisiana, and weighs in at two hundred and twenty-four pounds..." Hoskins said, "He is CORAL AVALON!" Coral climbed up the staircase and walked out onto the apron, before he looked out over the some fifty thousand fans in attendance for Players & Pawns. The fans cheered him even louder for acknowledging them, before Coral entered the ring via climbing over the top ropes at the corner, raising his right arm for all to see when he got to the top, before he jumped down into the ring. He acknowledged referee Patricia Grayson, a good friend of his, with a nod that pretty much said, "Just let me tear into this guy since there aren't any rules". Grayson shrugged a shrug that pretty much said, "No promises". Hoskins continued, "And his opponent..." "Change (In The House Of Flies)" by the Deftones. BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! The champion had arrived with the sound of three distinct pyrotechnic explosions, and DEAR JESUS, there was booing. There wasn't a man, woman, or child in the arena who wasn't booing "the Original" and his slutty wife, Julie Garvin, as he swaggered out from the curtains like he owned the place. Mind you, "the Original" Jeff Garvin had a good reason to feel as though he owned Safeco Field tonight, as he was the Action! Wrestling Champion. Not just the Action! Wrestling Champion, but the self-styled ORIGINAL Action! Champion. Abandoning the silver title worn by such stars as LLB and his brother-in-law, Joey Malone, Garvin disrespected every man that ever wore the silver version of the Action! Championship by trading it in for a large gold belt. But Garvin was never about respect. Even as a member, nay, LEADER of a team that wanted to usher in wrestling, Jeff Garvin had no respect for his peers. He had no respect for the legends. To him, he was the real legend, and all of the other legends were just idiots. To him, "Golden" Glen Miller, a twenty-plus year veteran of the ring who never gave up even despite a ruined left knee, was nothing. To him, a hardcore wrestler like KroW(or Levine) was so far below his level that he wasn't even visible. To him, Joey Malone, a man who spent eight years of his career giving everything he had for the fans, was nothing. To him, veterans like the Flying Frenchie and "Impulse" Brian James were mere lackies to his greatness. And, of course, to him, his four losses to the Kleptomaniac were merely lucky breaks. "He hails from Memphis, Tennessee, and weighs in at two hundred and forty-four pounds..." Hoskins said, then he said his next thing before he left the ring, "He is the ORIGINAL Action! Wrestling Champion... he is JEFF GARVIN!" Garvin wore his usual pink and green tights with "GARVIN" written on the front in white lettering. Garvin's wife dressed as little as she could afford given the chilly air of the arena. Neverthless, despite the fact that Wrestling 101 was banned from helping Jeff retain his title, Jeff has a psychological edge over his rival. Jeff had worked very hard to throw the Kleptomaniac off of his game, by attacking him psychologically rather than physically. Neverthless, Jeff could never underestimate Coral Avalon, because he had lost every single time that he had done so. Garvin stopped as he reached the ringside area, and glared at some ringside fans who were talking trash about "the Original". Something about sucking shit or some such nonsense. I didn't bother transcribing it, because the action of sucking shit just seemed kind of gross. Anyway, taking offense for his ego being bruised by the hurling of words, Garvin shouted at them, "HEY! I'll have you know that I am the greatest thing you have EVER seen, and I'm going to prove that ton-- OOF!" The OOF! in question came when Coral Avalon seized the brief moment of inattentiveness on Garvin's part to run into the ropes opposite of where Garvin was jawjacking with the fan. By the time Garvin turned around, it was too late, because the Kleptomaniac flew right over the top rope and on top of Garvin. A no-handed tope suicida, over the top rope. Fans gasped and applauded for the Kleptomaniac as he impacted the reigning Action! Wrestling champion. Then Coral did something that probably would have gotten him banned from the Danger School of Wrestling. Mind you, Coral never went to the Danger School of Wrestling. In fact, he got his degree in Kleptomania from the Anthason Academy of Ass-Kicking, before he transfered to the Malone Medical School of Mesa, where he majored in all-around wrestling and good natured behaviour. But the thing was that the Anthason Academy of Ass-Kicking and the Malone Medical School of Mesa allowed for punching people in their fucking faces until they either A) bled, B) stopped moving, or C) all of the above. The Danger School of Wrestling had this, carved in stone, above the stone statue of Sarah Michelle Gellar in its lobby: "THOU SHALT NOT THROW A PUNCH". But since Coral never went to the Danger School of Wrestling, he had no problems punching "the Original" Jeff Garvin in his face. Repeatedly and incessantly. Fans erupted positively for Coral, who stood up and looked down at Garvin. Patricia probably would have started a ten count had Coral not waved her off. Garvin hadn't suffered enough. "I'm sorry," she pleaded with everyone. And she frowned like this: :-( As that went on, Jeff Garvin collapsed in the corner, still holding his back in pain. He had expected a challenge in this match, but in no way, shape or form, did he expect Coral Avalon to be this pissed going into it. Coral's crimson-covered face was a mask of pain, and he dropped to his knees. Then Garvin glared at Grayson, shrugged, and then blasted Coral in his back a second time with the chair, before tossing the chair out to the floor. "Hey!" Trish screamed. "Stop it!" "Make me." Garvin coldly responded. Grayson glared sternly and disapprovingly at the Original. If only she had that splintered 2x4. "I'm not going to count him down!" she finally said, with her arms crossed across her chest. "Not now, you're not." And why not now, you ask? Because Garvin grabbed Coral by his hair and pulled him to his feet. It was only when Garvin applied the standing headscissors that the fans in Safeco Field mumured in horror. They knew what Jeff Garvin wanted. They knew that Coral Avalon was too weak to fight this off. They knew that, if this were Memphis, fans would be bowing their heads in silent awe at the mere sight of the... MEMPHIS DEATH CERTIFICATE. Coral landed right on his head upon the connection of the piledriver, and seemed to linger there, upside-down, for an uncomfortable second, before he collapsed to his right side. Fans were hushed to a stark silence, as Jeff Garvin started to stand, and then he glared at Grayson to count. Then he simply walked to a corner, waving off Coral Avalon's chances of ever returning to the mortal coil. Trish looked at Jeff. You know the vengeance stare in Kill Bill? It was like that. Her eyes shifted over to the fallen Avalon, and her heart nearly broke. She turned back to Garvin and glared. She had been suspended and fined already this year for not doing her job. She couldn't risk another. While she hated it, she had to count. She glared at Jeff all the while. One. Two. Jeff Garvin suddenly walked back over to Avalon and looked down on him. Three. But Garvin just wiped the blood from his face and swiped it down on Avalon's fallen body. Just to be a prick. Then he walked towards the corner again. Four. Grayson could have used it as an excuse to break the count, but she couldn't. She frowned so much at that. Five. Garvin prematurely raised his arms in celebration. I mean, why wouldn't he do that? He just hit his most devastating maneuver dead center in the ring. Why would he be worried? Six. Seven. Well, I'd start with the fact that Coral Avalon had stirred to his stomach. Eight. Then with the fact that Coral was slowly pushing up to his knees. Nine. And by the time Coral had wobbilly gotten to his feet, if only for a brief second before he fell over again, Jeff Garvin had gone absolutely nuts. He kicked at the ropes. He screamed, "NOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!!!!!!!!!!!". He could not possibly fucking believe that Coral Avalon just did that. He was just absolutely frustrated, now. He had done absolutely everything in his power, and still could not keep the Kleptomaniac down. Trish breathed a sigh of relief for Coral reaching his feet. With her hands on her hips, she looked over at Garvin and swiveled her head in that 'yeah, what do you think you that, bitch?' way. Garvin didn't see Trish's look, but felt it, and decided that he needed to show off what he thought of that. Bitch. He rolled out to the floor and collected the steel chair that had been previously kicked out of the ring by Grayson, after Coral hit his enzugiri into it. Garvin slid the chair into the ring, whereupon it came to a stop dead center in the ring. The crazed look in his eyes could only mean that he was planning on completely ending the career of the Kleptomaniac, once and for all. He rolled under the bottom rope, and walked over to the Kleptomaniac, who was on his hands and knees now. Garvin grabbed him by his hair and put him back in the standing headscissors, prepared to deliver the Memphis Death Certificate on the steel chair. "Coral," Trish whimpered in fear for the cute, but bloody, Avalon. Trish kicked the chair out of the way. Not smart. Garvin snapped his head toward Trish. Trish could have sworn that she saw Garvin's entire face turn red and steam was coming out of his ears. He probably would have hurt Trish right then and there, had Coral Avalon not done something in desperation, and for the good of humanity. He dropped to one knee and shot his right arm up, smashing Jeff Garvin in the balls. That sound you just heard would be sighs in relief that there would not be little Garvins running around the world any time soon. That second sound you just heard would be the simultaneous roar of the crowd, as Coral managed to stand up, underhooking Jeff Garvin's arms, and use every ounce of energy he had left in his body to execute the one move he was capable of using at this time. CURVEBALL~! Trish wiped the sweat from her forehead, thankfully for Coral's escape and the putting down of Jeff Garvin. To be on the safe side, she took the chair and dropped it out of the ring. And she took a deep breath, pumped her fist, and quietly said, "Come on, Coral." Yeah, she was biased. So sue her. She, however, had to make the mandatory ten count, but wondered in the back of her mind if these two men had anything left in their tanks after this hellacious match. One. Two. Three. Four. Avalon stirred, somehow. Garvin was still out on the mat after eating Scott Slugger's famed finishing maneuver. Five. Avalon rolled over onto his stomach and pushed himself up on his hands and knees, while Garvin continued to be down on the canvas. Neverthless, fans were holding their breaths to see if this would be enough. Six. They sighed a sigh of disappointment, as Garvin began to stir. Coral was on his knees, and needed only to make the move to his feet to get up, even though his entire body ached and didn't want to do that. Seven. Avalon was to his feet. Jeff Garvin was getting there. Fans were disappointed, but didn't stay disappointed, because Coral headed Jeff off at the past and kicked Garvin in the gut. From there, Coral turned around and grabbed Garvin's head. Right arm under the chin, left arm behind the head. Coral moved towards the ropes in a sprint, before he ran up them, springboarded off the top, and came back around. Acid Drop. Or, as Mike Ritz would call it... THE ASTRAL GAZER. Coral Avalon did not care for Mike Ritz in the very least, being that he was the man that retired "Golden" Glen Miller with a shady Pearl Harbor job. But on this night, the Astral Gazer proved to be a most effective way at keeping Garvin down. Grayson moved in to count, but Coral waved her off. He wasn't done, quite yet. He turned around and grabbed the ORIGINAL Action! Wrestling Champion by his hair, and slowly brought him to his feet. From there, he pulled the Original up into a fireman's carry. Coral Avalon stood there, dead center in the ring, with Garvin on his shoulders, looking out at the fifty thousand fans in attendance. This one was for Joey. MALONESTROM. The fireman's carry Michinoku Driver, or the Whiplash, or the Amityville Horror, that was perfected as a finishing maneuver for the former Action! Wrestling Heavyweight Champion had connected, dead center in the ring, and the fans were going berserk. Coral Avalon gingerly stood up, looked down at Garvin, then looked over at Grayson, and then elected for one last move. Coral stood over Garvin for the second time. The fans reacted enormously just to the positioning. They knew all too well that he wanted this move. Why wouldn't he? Coral tore off his left elbow pad and tossed it to the crowd. He did not wave his arms or anything, he simply took off. OFF THE FIRST SET OF ROPES. OFF THE OTHER SET OF ROPES. ... THE GOLDEN ELBOW!!! There wasn't a man, woman, or child who wasn't on their feet and cheering, upon the connection of Coral's right elbow colliding on Jeff Garvin's thick skull. Coral, while completely spent after hitting that last spurt of offense, was able to get to his feet and weakly lean against the turnbuckles, waving for Grayson to make the count. Patricia Grayson was getting shivers from the crowd reaction, but she made the count, much to her own giddiness. One. Two. Three. Four. Five. Six. Garvin stirred. Seven. Garvin started to slowly crawl to his stomach, completely dazed, but more or less, he had a feeling in his gut that he knew what had just happened to him. Eight. When that realization hit, he felt incredibly, incredibly pissed off. Nine. And that's when, JUST before Grayson could count to ten, Garvin got to his feet again. "Goddamit," she muttered not-so-under her breath. She wanted to just jump up and down, shouting "TEN! TEN! TEN!" But that would be wrong. So she frowned, scowled, and capped it off with a glare. But all the while, Jeff Garvin saw red. Not just the blood that was streaming down his face, based on Avalon's Golden Elbow further opening his wound. But also just with the fact that he was just hit with the move that he hated more than anything else the entire world. When Avalon moved in to possibly hit him with something else, maybe the Ratings Spike, that's when Garvin exploded and kicked Avalon in the nuts as hard as he possibly could. Fans booed. They didn't like that one bit. But, hell, it was no holds barred. Grayson grimaced and wanted to just console Coral after seeing Jeff crush his private area. Not in that way, you perverts. Garvin was dizzy, confused, and overall pissed off. He looked around for the steel chair, unaware that Patricia Grayson had tossed it from the ring again. He looked to the outside, shrugged, and slid out before grabbing the steel chair that he had intended to use for the purposes of caving in Patricia Grayson's skull earlier. That wasn't his intention this time, because every ounce of focus that he had was centered on Avalon. He slid into the ring, wielding the chair, before he stepped on Coral's stomach and unleashed his fury. SMACK! SMACK! CHAIR. SMACK! SMACK! YOU. SMACK! SMACK! FACE. SMMAAAACCCCK! By the time Garvin was finished, the weapon was but a twisted mesh of steel that had once been a steel chair. During the onslaught of steel chair shots, the back end of the thing flew off into the crowd. The weapon was completely bent out of shape, and Garvin simply tossed it away when he was done. But Garvin was not done yet. Hell no. Not by a long shot. Trish was near tears after the undauntingly vicious assault against Coral Avalon. She wanted to take the chair and beat on Jeff Garvin, then his wife, and then on any children they may have in the future. (Which wasn't bloody likely.) But what could she do? She was but a lowly referee with perky breasts and a sparkling personality. However, she would be beside herself momentarilly, as Garvin maneuvered himself to Coral's legs and picked them up. He stepped through Coral's legs with his right leg, wrapped them around that right leg, and turned Coral Avalon over. HAMMER JAMMER. "No," was all Trish could whimper while shaking her head. If only she could just kick Jeff in the face. Just once. They wouldn't get mad at her for that would they? I mean, look at what he's doing to poor Coral Avalon! Trish was furious and sad and hurt and all sorts of emotions. Garvin did not give a flying fuck what Grayson's problems were. He was simply content with making the Kleptomaniac suffer. He pulled back on Coral's legs and arched back as far as he could, screaming bloody murder while Coral grimaced and cried out in pain. No, Garvin didn't care that submissions didn't count for anything in a Last Man Standing match. He cared that this could break the Kleptomaniac's legs and end his career and bid for HIS Action! Championship. Little did he know, however, that he had not broken the Kleptomaniac's spirits quite yet. Coral slowly crawled toward the bottom ropes. Garvin couldn't believe it, but he didn't have the proper footing to drag Coral back to the center of the ring. Eventually, through much crawling, and torment, Coral grabbed hold of the bottom ropes and held on to them. "Oh, thank god," Trish breathed. She walked over and started tapping on Jeff's arm. "Let go! He's in the ropes!" Of course, the ropes didn't count for shit in this sort of matchup. Garvin shot a death glance at Trish and seethed two very distinct words, "MAKE. ME." Trish couldn't really do anything to make him. Well, she could slap him. SLAP! It didn't cause Garvin to release the hold, but it did piss him the fuck off. Now, Trish was scared for her life. But Garvin just looked sternly at Trish. A smile crept across his face. He didn't attack Grayson. He simply applied more pressure to Coral. Coral cried out in pain, and knew that he was in big trouble. Garvin wasn't releasing the hold, and the ropes would've been his own salvation. And yet, Coral remembered a way to break the hold, however painful it may be for him to try it. He grabbed hold of the second ropes and climbed up. This increased the pressure of the hold, but it also made it more difficult for Garvin to maintain it. In fact, when Coral climbed up to the top ropes, Garvin found it impossible to keep the hold applied without being in an awkward position, himself, and he was forced to break the hold. Garvin cursed loudly and audibly. He glared at Patricia Grayson, very, very displeased with her behaviour in this contest so far. Neverthless, Coral was still leaning against the ropes, in prone position. Garvin simply turned towards him and hooked in the position that was needed for him to execute... THE ORIGINAL SLAM! DENIED! Avalon landed behind Garvin and slammed his elbow into Garvin's back. The same one that he had been working over early on in the match. Garvin cried out in pain, and this enabled Coral to hook in an inverted facelock. It was obvious that Coral was going for the third and last one of his main three signature maneuvers. RATINGS SPIK-- NEGATIVE! Garvin managed to counter into a waistlock, and simply shoved Coral into the ropes. When Coral returned, Garvin tried a tilt-a-whirl something or other, but Coral landed on his feet before he could do anything with it. Then Coral kicked him in the stomach and tried his own whip. But Garvin reversed into his own, and when Coral came back, he regretted it. FIST OF STONE. CRUNCH. That sound you just heard would be Garvin unleashing the stiffest punch seen in A!W history to the jaw of Coral Avalon. Coral Avalon was down. Coral Avalon might have been out. Garvin waved his hand frantically, trying to cool off the pain that had blasted his hand. Trish covered her eyes. It was like Coral had been shot in the face. She couldn't look. She didn't want to look. She just wanted to go home, crawl in her bed, and hide underneath the covers. Garvin had to shout at her, "COUNT HIM!" Her lower lip quivered as she looked up at Jeff with water in her eyes. She wanted to shake her head and be all defiant. Make a stand and just walk out, perhaps. But she couldn't. She set her jaw. She had faith in Coral. He would get up. When she began her count, she whispered to herself, "Come on, Coral. Please get up." One. Garvin stared at Coral Avalon intently. There was no more arrogance emenating from the Original One. Just a strong desire to put the Kleptomaniac down and KEEP HIM THERE. Two. Even then, Jeff Garvin wanted to be sure. He rolled to the outside while Trish was counting, and collected the steel chair that wasn't destroyed. The same one that he had attempted to use the Memphis Death Certificate on earlier in the contest. Three. Four. Sliding the chair into the ring and following it in, Garvin simply watched and waited for Avalon to move. Five. He did. Six. Grayson was beside herself with delight, but Garvin calmly slid the chair into position with his foot, and waited for the Kleptomaniac to even dare getting up. Seven. Avalon was on his hands and knees, trying desperately to get to his feet. Eight. Avalon was going to make it to his feet, now on one knee, though still holding his face in pain from the impact of the Fist of Stone. Nine. Avalon was back up. Trish pumped her fist and in the middle of saying, "Yes!" Garvin kicked Avalon in the gut.... He put Coral in the standing headscissors, and before Trish could move to kick it out of the way THIS time, Garvin had executed it. MEMPHIS DEATH CERTIFICATE, NUMBER TWO. A jumping piledriver that had spiked the Kleptomaniac like a javelin onto the chair. Again, Coral seemed to linger there as if he were imbedded there, before he toppled over to his left. Completely and totally out of this contest, now. Trish was shaking her head. She didn't want to believe it. What she wanted to do, as did a lot of those watching, was pick up the chair and start bashing Garvin in the head with it. But she couldn't. Not for fear of losing her job, she just couldn't. She couldn't move. Garvin stood up, his face a crimson mess, but he was satisfied. He noticed, however, that Grayson wasn't counting, and pointed at her, "COUNT!" "RUN!" she screamed inside her head. "RUN!" She wanted to get out of there. She didn't want to have to count Coral Avalon down, and stand there as Jeff Garvin paraded around his fallen body. Two tears rolled down her cheeks. She opened her mouth once, but nothing came out. No sound escaped. Her mouth had dried up. She swallowed and wiped the tears from her cheeks. She looked down at Coral. Back up to Jeff. She narrowed her eyes, full of hurt as they were, and just shook her head in disgust at Jeff before saying, "One." Two. Three. Four. Coral didn't move a muscle. Five. He had to have been unconscious. Six. The fans were still trying to cheer Coral to his feet by some miracle. Seven. But Patricia felt all hope leave her, as Coral still hadn't even tried to get up. This match was as good as over. Eight. Nine. Ten. The entire arena deflated as Trish called out, numbly, ten. She just stared at the mat. Not at Coral. But at a single blood stain on the canvas. But she wasn't really looking at it. More through it. Her mind was swimming with saddness. Her body catonic. It was nearly a minute into "Change(In the House of Flies)" before she blinked and looked around the ring. When she regained focus and saw the bloody and unconscious form of Coral Avalon laying on the mat, the tears came fast and hard. She covered her face in her hands and dropped to her knees. Jeff Garvin had collected his title, and was celebrating. For nearly a year, the shadow of Coral Avalon had been looming over him, threatening to destroy him. But tonight, he had finally proven to himself that he could beat Coral Avalon, however dishonorably he did so. He left, holding the championship high over his head. EMTs soon entered the scene to check on the condition of the former Bantam champion, trying to determine if he could walk out under his own power or not. When they determined that he probably should've do that, they started to get out the stretcher. Trish had forced herself to crawl over to Coral. "I'm sorry, Coral," she wept. The EMTs were telling her to get back so that they could load him up. The EMTs loaded Coral Avalon up onto the stretcher, much to the displeasure of the Seattle crowd, and especially Trish. Before getting out of the ring, Grayson looked back at Jeff, sneering. She shook her head and muttered, "Bastard." Outside the ring, she followed alongside the stretcher. As they went up the aisle, Julie Garvin was making her way back down, in a whole new trashy outfit. She walked with that 'my hubbie's the champ, bitch, and I'm gonna go fuck some guys tonight' swagger. She and Trish locked eyes as they passed. Julie had a look of 'that's what you get, ho', and Trish's was all 'I'm gonna rip your nipples off and ram them up your anal cavity' with her's. It was an unhappy medium. Trish looked into the ambulance and back at the line of people watching. She spotted E.M. Pleh standing in the distance, with her hands on her hips. Trish wiped away some of her tears, sighed, and climbed in. When Action! Wrestling's longest feud started all the way back in September, nobody knew how far it would go. One man walked away the champion. The other was carried out. Now the only question was: Who would be the next victim of Jeff Garvin? Outcome: Garvin via Knockout; Retains A Title. © Action Wrestling 2002-2003
